Notably, we have used WarpL to successfully diagnose six previously unknown performance issues in Wasmtime.

</details>


### [197] [On the Effectiveness of Context Compression for Repository-Level Tasks: An Empirical Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13725)
*Jia Feng,Zhanyue Qin,Cuiyun Gao,Ruiqi Wang,Chaozheng Wang,Yingwei Ma,Xiaoyuan Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了代码仓库级别智能任务中的上下文压缩方法，将其分为三类范式，并验证了其在代码补全和生成任务中的有效性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理多文件长上下文时，关键信息可能被噪声掩盖、截断或导致延迟增加，而上下文压缩可以缓解这些问题。

Method: 将八种上下文压缩方法归类为三类范式：离散标记序列、连续潜在向量和视觉标记，并在代码补全和生成任务中进行评估。

Result: 在4倍压缩下，连续潜在向量方法的性能比完整上下文高28.3%（BLEU分数），所有范式均降低了推理成本，视觉和文本压缩在高比率下可减少50%的端到端延迟。

Conclusion: 上下文压缩是可行的方法，并为范式选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Repository-level code intelligence tasks require large language models (LLMs) to process long, multi-file contexts. Such inputs introduce three challenges: crucial context can be obscured by noise, truncated due to limited windows, and increased inference latency. Context compression mitigates these risks by condensing inputs. While studied in NLP, its applicability to code tasks remains largely unexplored. We present the first systematic empirical study of context compression for repository-level code intelligence, organizing eight methods into three paradigms: discrete token sequences, continuous latent vectors, and visual tokens. We evaluate them on code completion and generation, measuring performance and efficiency. Results show context compression is effective: at 4x compression, continuous latent vector methods surpass full-context performance by up to 28.3% in BLEU score, indicating they filter noise rather than just truncating. On efficiency, all paradigms reduce inference cost. Both visual and text-based compression achieve up to 50% reduction in end-to-end latency at high ratios, approaching the cost of inference without repository context. These findings establish context compression as a viable approach and provide guidance for paradigm selection.

</details>


### [198] [Zero-shot Evaluation of Deep Learning for Java Code Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13783)
*Thomas S. Heinze*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DL-based克隆检测器在零样本评估中泛化能力不足，传统工具表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究DL-based clone detectors在Java代码中的泛化能力。

Method: 复制并评估五种基于DL的克隆检测器（如CodeBERT和FA-AST+GMN）在零样本评估场景中的性能。

Result: 模型对未见代码的泛化能力有限，NiCad在某些情况下优于DL-based检测器。

Conclusion: DL-based clone detectors在零样本评估场景中的泛化能力有限，传统工具如NiCad甚至表现更优。

Abstract: Deep Learning (DL) is becoming more and more widespread in clone detection, motivated by achieving near-perfect performance for this task. In particular in case of semantic code clones, which share only limited syntax but implement the same or similar functionality, Deep Learning appears to outperform conventional tools. In this paper, we want to investigate the generalizability of DL-based clone detectors for Java. We therefore replicate and evaluate the performance of five state-of-the-art DL-based clone detectors, including Transformers like CodeBERT and single-task models like FA-AST+GMN, in a zero-shot evaluation scenario, where we train/fine-tune and evaluate on different datasets and functionalities. Our experiments demonstrate that the models' generalizability to unseen code is limited. Further analysis reveals that the conventional clone detector NiCad even outperforms the DL-based clone detectors in such a zero-shot evaluation scenario.

</details>


### [199] [A Universal Textual Merge Strategy Based on Tokens for Version Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13813)
*Qiqi Jason Gu,Mikoláš Janota*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summer是一种新型的基于文本令牌的合并算法，独立于文档格式，通过令牌级变化建模和规则应用，减少合并冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于行的合并算法在重构或并行编辑时容易产生虚假冲突，而现有语法和语义感知方法存在格式丢失、语言依赖和灵活性不足的问题。

Method: 将文本划分为令牌，将分支中的令牌级变化转化为字符串重写规则和移动规则，并应用于另一分支的文本以构建合并。

Result: 在ConflictBench基准测试中，Summer在Java和非Java文件上实现了36%的准确率（与开发者合并完全一致），语义准确率排名第二。

Conclusion: Summer通过令牌级规则建模，提供了一种语言无关的合并方法，显著减少了虚假冲突，并在实际场景中表现优异。

Abstract: Merging is a core operation in version control systems such as Git, but traditional line-based algorithms often yield spurious conflicts, particularly in the presence of refactorings or parallel edits. While syntax- and semantics-aware merging approaches can reduce conflicts, they introduce drawbacks such as loss of formatting, dependence on language-specific parsers, and limited flexibility across heterogeneous artifacts. To address this gap, we present Summer, a novel textual token-based merge algorithm independent of document formats. Dividing text into tokens, our approach formulates token-level changes in one branch into string-rewriting rules and move rules, and applies these rules to the text of the other branch to construct a merge. Despite being independent on programming languages, our move rules model extracting and inlining functions. We evaluated Summer on ConflictBench, a large benchmark of real-world merge scenarios, comparing it with five pioneering merge tools across Java and non-Java files. Experimental results show that Summer achieved the highest 36% accuracy in reproducing merges verbatim identical to developers', and ranked second in semantic accuracy.

</details>


### [200] [Sentiment analysis for software engineering: How far can zero-shot learning (ZSL) go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13826)
*Reem Alfayez,Manal Binkhonain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了零样本学习（ZSL）在软件工程情感分析中的应用潜力，以解决标注数据稀缺的问题。实验表明，结合专家标签的ZSL技术可以达到与微调Transformer模型相当的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域的情感分析需要专业工具，但标注数据获取困难，ZSL可能提供解决方案。

Method: 通过实证实验评估多种ZSL技术（嵌入、NLI、TARS、生成模型）的性能，并与微调Transformer模型对比。

Result: ZSL技术（尤其是结合专家标签的嵌入或生成模型）性能接近微调Transformer模型，但分类错误主要由标注主观性和极性事实引起。

Conclusion: ZSL为软件工程情感分析提供了一种减少依赖标注数据的可行方案。

Abstract: Sentiment analysis in software engineering focuses on understanding emotions expressed in software artifacts. Previous research highlighted the limitations of applying general off-the-shelf sentiment analysis tools within the software engineering domain and indicated the need for specialized tools tailored to various software engineering contexts. The development of such tools heavily relies on supervised machine learning techniques that necessitate annotated datasets. Acquiring such datasets is a substantial challenge, as it requires domain-specific expertise and significant effort. Objective: This study explores the potential of ZSL to address the scarcity of annotated datasets in sentiment analysis within software engineering Method:} We conducted an empirical experiment to evaluate the performance of various ZSL techniques, including embedding-based, NLI-based, TARS-based, and generative-based ZSL techniques. We assessed the performance of these techniques under different labels setups to examine the impact of label configurations. Additionally, we compared the results of the ZSL techniques with state-of-the-art fine-tuned transformer-based models. Finally, we performed an error analysis to identify the primary causes of misclassifications. Results: Our findings demonstrate that ZSL techniques, particularly those combining expert-curated labels with embedding-based or generative-based models, can achieve macro-F1 scores comparable to fine-tuned transformer-based models. The error analysis revealed that subjectivity in annotation and polar facts are the main contributors to ZSL misclassifications. Conclusion: This study demonstrates the potential of ZSL for sentiment analysis in software engineering. ZSL can provide a solution to the challenge of annotated dataset scarcity by reducing reliance on annotated dataset.

</details>


### [201] [Towards Enabling An Artificial Self-Construction Software Life-cycle via Autopoietic Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13934)
*Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,David Nader Palacio,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了自组织软件架构的潜力，提出将基础模型与自组织架构结合以实现软件的自主演化，并呼吁研究新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件维护和演化过程成本高且可靠性不足，基础模型的出现为自主软件行为提供了新机会。

Method: 通过分析传统维护方法的局限性，提出将基础模型与自组织架构结合，以实现软件的自主构建。

Result: 本文未提出具体解决方案，但指出了实现自组织软件的核心挑战，包括基础模型推理单元的集成和新架构范式的建立。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于自组织架构（如Psi-Arch）的新范式，旨在推动软件工程向自主构建和演化的方向发展，并呼吁进一步研究以实现这一目标。

Abstract: Software engineering research has focused on automating maintenance and evolution processes to reduce costs and improve reliability. The emergence of foundation models (FMs) with strong code understanding and reasoning abilities offers new opportunities for autonomous software behavior. Inspired by Artificial Life (ALife), we propose a fundamental shift in the Software Development Life-Cycle (SDLC) by introducing self-construction mechanisms that enable software to evolve and maintain autonomously. This position paper explores the potential of Autopoietic Architectures, specifically Psi-Arch, as a foundational framework for self-constructing software. We first analyze the limitations of traditional maintenance approaches and identify gaps in current SDLC automation. Subsequently, we outline the core challenges in achieving self-construction, including the integration of foundation-model-based reasoning units and the establishment of novel architectural paradigms. Although this paper does not present a definitive solution, it seeks to catalyze discourse and inspire research toward a new paradigm in software engineering, one in which self-constructing software represents the next frontier in SDLC automation.

</details>


### [202] [CollabCoder: Plan-Code Co-Evolution via Collaborative Decision-Making for Efficient Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13946)
*Duy Tung Doan,Quang Huy Phung,Dzung Nguyen,Khac-Hoai Nam Bui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CollabCoder 是一种新颖的 Plan-Code 协同进化框架，通过动态多智能体协作提升代码生成质量，显著减少计算开销并提高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体框架在代码生成中存在静态规划、孤立执行、高计算开销和适应复杂任务能力不足的问题。

Method: 设计了计划模块与代码模块之间的协作决策过程，动态决定调试过程中执行哪个模块。

Result: 在 LiveCodeBench 和 xCodeEval 基准测试中，性能提升 11-20%，同时平均减少每次执行的 API 调用次数 4-10 次。

Conclusion: CollabCoder 在代码质量和鲁棒性上表现优异，计算效率更高，尤其在复杂任务中优势更明显。

Abstract: Automated code generation remains a persistent challenge in software engineering, as conventional multi-agent frameworks are often constrained by static planning, isolated execution, high computational overhead, and limited adaptability to complex tasks. This paper introduces CollabCoder, a novel Plan-Code Co-Evolution framework that improves code generation through dynamic multi-agent collaboration. The core idea is to design a collaborative decision-making process between the plan module and the code module to decide which module should be executed for the debugging process. Extensive experiments on widely used benchmarks demonstrate that CollabCoder consistently improves code quality and robustness across tasks. Importantly, CollabCoder achieves performance comparable to or exceeding current state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead, with efficiency gains becoming more pronounced as benchmark difficulty increases. On the more challenging LiveCodeBench and xCodeEval benchmarks, our approach improves performance by 11-20% over strong baselines while reducing the number of API calls by an average of 4-10 per execution.

</details>


### [203] [Learned or Memorized ? Quantifying Memorization Advantage in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13997)
*Djiré Albérick Euraste,Kaboré Abdoul Kader,Jordan Samhi,Earl T. Barr,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种量化代码大语言模型记忆优势的方法，发现记忆风险因任务和模型而异，并强调了安全场景中评估协议的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏关于用于训练大语言模型的代码数据集的透明度，难以检测、评估和缓解数据泄漏问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于扰动的方法来量化代码大语言模型中的记忆优势，定义为已见和未见输入之间的性能差距。

Result: 评估了8个开源代码大语言模型在19个基准测试中的表现，发现不同模型和任务的敏感性差异显著。例如，StarCoder在某些基准测试中敏感性较高（高达0.8），而QwenCoder则较低（大多低于0.4）。此外，CVEFixes和Defects4J这两个被广泛讨论的基准测试显示出较低的记忆优势。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，记忆风险高度依赖于任务和模型，强调了在安全相关场景中需要更强的评估协议。

Abstract: The lack of transparency about code datasets used to train large language models (LLMs) makes it difficult to detect, evaluate, and mitigate data leakage. We present a perturbation-based method to quantify memorization advantage in code LLMs, defined as the performance gap between likely seen and unseen inputs.
  We evaluate 8 open-source code LLMs on 19 benchmarks across four task families: code generation, code understanding, vulnerability detection, and bug fixing. Sensitivity patterns vary widely across models and tasks. For example, StarCoder reaches high sensitivity on some benchmarks (up to 0.8), while QwenCoder remains lower (mostly below 0.4), suggesting differences in generalization behavior. Task categories also differ: code summarization tends to show low sensitivity, whereas test generation is substantially higher.
  We then analyze two widely discussed benchmarks, CVEFixes and Defects4J, often suspected of leakage. Contrary to common concerns, both show low memorization advantage across models: CVEFixes remains below 0.1, and Defects4J is lower than other program repair benchmarks. These results suggest that, for these datasets, models may rely more on learned generalization than direct memorization.
  Overall, our findings provide evidence that memorization risk is highly task- and model-dependent, and highlight the need for stronger evaluation protocols, especially in security-focused settings.

</details>


### [204] [Analysis of Commit Signing on Github](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14014)
*Abubakar Sadiq Shittu,John Sadik,Farzin Gholamrezae,Scott Ruoti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过开发者行为的大规模分析，揭示了当前提交签名在实际应用中的不足，发现仅有少数开发者真正使用签名，且签名行为缺乏持续性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示提交签名在软件供应链安全中的实际应用情况，超越以往局限于单个仓库或精选样本的研究，提供更全面的开发者行为视角。

Method: 基于可复制性理论，研究从仓库抽样转向开发者抽样，跟踪了71,694名活跃GitHub用户的行为，覆盖了1,600万次提交和874,198个仓库。

Result: 研究发现，排除平台生成的签名后，仅有不到6%的开发者真正使用签名，且签名行为缺乏持续性。此外，许多签名密钥未上传或已过期，导致验证失败。

Conclusion: 当前提交签名无法作为生态系统规模的可靠来源信号，论文提出了改进建议以填补这一差距。

Abstract: Commit signing is widely promoted as a foundation of software supply-chain security, yet prior work has studied it through the lens of individual repositories or curated project samples, missing the broader picture of how developers behave across an entire platform. Grounded in replicability theory, we vary the sampling unit from repositories to individual developers, following 71,694 active GitHub users, defined as accounts that have authored at least one commit, across all their repositories and their entire commit history, spanning 16 million commits and 874,198 repositories. This platform-wide, user-centric view reveals a fundamental gap that repository sampling cannot detect. The ecosystem's apparent high signing adoption rate is an illusion. Once platform-generated signatures are excluded, fewer than 6% of developers have ever signed a commit themselves, and the vast majority of apparent signers have never signed outside a web browser. Among the minority who do sign locally, signing rarely persists over time or across repositories, and roughly one in eight developer-managed signatures fails verification because signing keys are never uploaded to GitHub. Examining the key registry, we find that expired keys are almost never revoked and more than a quarter of users carry at least one dead key. Together, these findings reveal that commit signing as practiced today cannot serve as a dependable provenance signal at ecosystem scale, and we offer concrete recommendations for closing that gap.

</details>


### [205] [Log-based vs Graph-based Approaches to Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14019)
*Mathis Nguyen,Mohamed Ali Lajnef*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 比较了基于日志的编码器架构（如BERT）和图模型（如GNN）在自动故障诊断中的表现，发现结合两者性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有日志分析方法忽略事件间的结构上下文（如父子依赖、分支或时间特征），而图模型可能提供更好的解决方案。

Method: 在TraceBench和BGL数据集上评估了日志编码器和图模型的性能，包括异常检测和故障类型分类。

Result: 纯图模型表现不如编码器基线，但结合日志编码器和图模型的混合架构表现最佳。

Conclusion: 图增强架构在特定条件下优于传统日志分析方法。

Abstract: Modern distributed systems generate large volumes of logs that can be analyzed to support essential AIOps tasks such as fault diagnosis, which plays a crucial role in maintaining system reliability. Most existing approaches rely on log-based models that treat logs as linear sequences of events. However, such representations discard the structural context between events that are often present in execution logs, such as parent-child dependencies, fan-out (branching), or temporal features. To better capture these relationships, recent works on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) suggest that representing logs as graphs offers a promising alternative. Building on these observations, this paper conducts a comparative study of log-based encoder architectures (e.g., BERT) and graph-based models (e.g., GNNs) for automated fault diagnosis. We evaluate our models on TraceBench, a trace-oriented log dataset, and on BGL, a more traditional system log dataset, covering both anomaly detection and fault type classification. Our results show that graph-only models fail to outperform encoder baselines. However, integrating learned representations from log encoders into graph-based models achieves the strongest overall performance. These findings highlight conditions under which graph-augmented architectures can outperform traditional log-based approaches.

</details>


### [206] [Large Language Models to Enhance Business Process Modeling: Past, Present, and Future Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14034)
*João Bettencourt,Sérgio Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文回顾了AI驱动的方法，特别是大型语言模型（LLMs），用于将自然语言转换为BPMN流程模型的研究现状，揭示了从传统方法向LLM架构的转变，并指出了语义正确性、评估碎片化等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨生成式人工智能（如LLMs）在自动化或辅助业务流程建模任务中的应用效果及其局限性。

Method: 采用结构化文献综述策略，识别并分析相关研究，分类现有方法，研究LLMs在文本到模型转换中的作用及评估实践。

Result: 研究发现从基于规则的传统NLP方法转向依赖提示工程、中间表示和迭代优化机制的LLM架构的明显趋势，但存在语义正确性和评估碎片化等问题。

Conclusion: 未来研究方向包括通过检索增强生成（RAG）整合上下文知识、开发交互式建模架构及更全面的标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative Artificial Intelligence, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), have stimulated growing interest in automating or assisting Business Process Modeling tasks using natural language. Several approaches have been proposed to transform textual process descriptions into BPMN and related workflow models. However, the extent to which these approaches effectively support complex process modeling in organizational settings remains unclear. This article presents a literature review of AI-driven methods for transforming natural language into BPMN process models, with a particular focus on the role of LLMs. Following a structured review strategy, relevant studies were identified and analyzed to classify existing approaches, examine how LLMs are integrated into text-to-model pipelines, and investigate the evaluation practices used to assess generated models. The analysis reveals a clear shift from rule-based and traditional NLP pipelines toward LLM-based architectures that rely on prompt engineering, intermediate representations, and iterative refinement mechanisms. While these approaches significantly expand the capabilities of automated process model generation, the literature also exposes persistent challenges related to semantic correctness, evaluation fragmentation, reproducibility, and limited validation in real-world organizational contexts. Based on these findings, this review identifies key research gaps and discusses promising directions for future research, including the integration of contextual knowledge through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), its integration with LLMs, the development of interactive modeling architectures, and the need for more comprehensive and standardized evaluation frameworks.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [207] [Event Tensor: A Unified Abstraction for Compiling Dynamic Megakernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13327)
*Hongyi Jin,Bohan Hou,Guanjie Wang,Ruihang Lai,Jinqi Chen,Zihao Ye,Yaxing Cai,Yixin Dong,Xinhao Cheng,Zhihao Zhang,Yilong Zhao,Yingyi Huang,Lijie Yang,Jinchen Jiang,Gabriele Oliaro,Jianan Ji,Xupeng Miao,Vinod Grover,Todd C. Mowry,Zhihao Jia,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Event Tensor提出了一种动态megakernel的统一编译器抽象，通过静态和动态调度优化，显著提升了LLM推理的性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU工作负载（尤其是大型语言模型推理）面临内核启动开销和粗粒度同步问题，限制了内核间并行性。现有megakernel技术虽能融合多个操作符，但难以处理动态形状和数据依赖计算。

Method: 提出Event Tensor抽象，编码任务间的依赖关系，支持动态形状和数据依赖。基于此，Event Tensor Compiler (ETC)通过静态和动态调度生成高性能持久化内核。

Result: ETC在LLM推理中实现了最先进的延迟性能，并显著降低了系统预热开销。

Conclusion: Event Tensor为动态megakernel提供了一种高效的编译器抽象，解决了现有技术的局限性，适用于复杂工作负载。

Abstract: Modern GPU workloads, especially large language model (LLM) inference, suffer from kernel launch overheads and coarse synchronization that limit inter-kernel parallelism. Recent megakernel techniques fuse multiple operators into a single persistent kernel to eliminate launch gaps and expose inter-kernel parallelism, but struggle to handle dynamic shapes and data-dependent computation in real workloads. We present Event Tensor, a unified compiler abstraction for dynamic megakernels. Event Tensor encodes dependencies between tiled tasks, and enables first-class support for both shape and data-dependent dynamism. Built atop this abstraction, our Event Tensor Compiler (ETC) applies static and dynamic scheduling transformations to generate high-performance persistent kernels. Evaluations show that ETC achieves state-of-the-art LLM serving latency while significantly reducing system warmup overhead.

</details>


### [208] [PackSELL: A Sparse Matrix Format for Precision-Agnostic High-Performance SpMV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13433)
*Kengo Suzuki,Takeshi Iwashita*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PackSELL是一种新的稀疏矩阵格式，通过delta编码和打包方案优化GPU上的稀疏矩阵-向量乘法（SpMV），支持灵活的数据表示并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏矩阵格式在GPU上的性能和数据表示灵活性不足，需要一种更高效且支持多样化数据表示的格式。

Method: 基于SELL格式，引入delta编码和打包方案，将索引-delta-值对存储为单个字，减少内存占用和数据移动，并支持非IEEE格式。

Result: 在FP16配置下，PackSELL的SpMV内核性能比cuSPARSE SELL高1.63倍；使用自定义格式时，性能优于FP16 cuSPARSE且达到FP32精度。稀疏线性求解器（如PCG）性能提升2.09倍。

Conclusion: PackSELL通过创新的数据表示和存储方案，显著提升了GPU上稀疏矩阵计算的性能和灵活性。

Abstract: We propose a new sparse matrix format, PackSELL, designed to support diverse data representations and enable efficient sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV) on GPUs. Building on sliced ELLPACK (SELL), PackSELL incorporates delta encoding of column indices and a novel packing scheme that stores each index-delta-value pair in a single word, thereby reducing memory footprint and data movement. This design further enables fine-grained control over the bit allocation between deltas and values, allowing flexible data representations, including non-IEEE formats. Experimental results show that, when configured for half precision (FP16), the PackSELL-based SpMV kernel outperforms the cuSPARSE SELL-based kernel by up to $1.63\times$. Moreover, with configurations using customized formats, PackSELL achieves FP32-level accuracy while exceeding the performance of FP16 cuSPARSE. These benefits extend to sparse linear solvers; for example, a mixed-precision preconditioned conjugate gradient (PCG) solver using PackSELL achieves up to a $2.09\times$ speedup over the standard full-precision PCG.

</details>


### [209] [SAKURAONE: An Open Ethernet-Based AI HPC System and Its Observed Workload Dynamics in a Single-Tenant LLM Development Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13600)
*Fumikazu Konishi,Yuuki Tsubouchi,Hirofumi Tsuruta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SAKURAONE是一个高性能计算集群，用于大型语言模型训练，展示了开放网络技术的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 展示SAKURAONE在HPC领域的性能和应用潜力，特别是在开放网络技术和GPU集群动态利用方面的优势。

Method: 基于KOKARYOKU PHY裸金属GPU平台构建，采用800 GbE网络和SONiC技术，支持大规模并行计算。

Result: 在ISC 2025 TOP500中排名49，性能达33.95 PFLOP/s（HPL），并展示了GPU资源使用的动态变化。

Conclusion: SAKURAONE证明了开放网络技术在高性能计算中的可行性，并为GPU集群的动态资源利用提供了实际案例。

Abstract: SAKURAONE is a managed high performance computing (HPC) cluster developed and operated by the SAKURA Internet Research Center. It builds on the KOKARYOKU PHY bare metal GPU platform and is optimized for advanced workloads, including large language model (LLM) training. In ISC 2025 TOP500, SAKURAONE is ranked 49th by HPL and is the only top 100 system that uses a fully open networking stack - 800 GbE with SONiC - demonstrating the scalability of vendor-neutral technology. Measured performance is 33.95 PFLOP/s (HPL Rmax), 396.295 TFLOP/s (HPCG), and 339.86 PFLOP/s on HPL-MxP with FP8. The system consists of 100 nodes, each with eight NVIDIA H100 GPUs and a 2 PB all-flash Lustre file system, interconnected via a rail-optimized 800 GbE leaf-spine fabric with RoCEv2. Through exclusive use by a single research project, we observed the characteristics of development-related jobs. Consistent with previous HPC studies, small-scale jobs dominated in number, while a few large-scale jobs accounted for most GPU resource time. As the project progressed, resource use shifted from large-scale to mid-scale jobs, reflecting a transition from initial large-scale training to iterative refinement. These observations illustrate the real-world utilization dynamics of GPU clusters under unified project workloads.

</details>


### [210] [OffloadFS: Leveraging Disaggregated Storage for Computation Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13743)
*Sungho Moon,Daegyu Han,Hera Koo,Sangeun Chae,Duck-Ho Bae,Euiseong Seo,Beomseok Nam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OffloadFS是一个用户级文件系统，支持将IO密集型任务卸载到分解存储节点或计算节点，无需分布式锁管理，显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分解存储系统中的NVMeoF技术具有大量计算和内存资源，但通常未充分利用。OffloadFS旨在利用这些资源进行近数据处理。

Method: 开发了OffloadFS文件系统，优化缓存管理并减少线程干扰。基于此，构建了OffloadDB和OffloadPrep，分别支持RocksDB和机器学习预处理任务的卸载。

Result: 与OCFS2相比，OffloadFS将RocksDB和机器学习预处理任务的性能分别提升至3.36倍和1.85倍。

Conclusion: OffloadFS通过任务卸载和优化缓存管理，显著提升了分解存储系统的性能，证明了近数据处理的潜力。

Abstract: Disaggregated storage systems improve resource utilization and enable independent scaling of storage and compute resources by separating storage resources from computing resources in data centers. NVMe over fabrics (NVMeoF) is a key technology that underpins the functionality and benefits of disaggregated storage systems. While NVMeoF inherently possesses substantial computing and memory capacity, these resources are often underutilized for tasks beyond simple I/O delegation. This study proposes OffloadFS, a user-level file system that enables offloaded IO-intensive tasks primarily to a disaggregated storage node for near-data processing, with the option to offload to peer compute nodes as well, without the need for distributed lock management. OffloadFS optimizes cache management by reducing interference between threads performing distinct I/O operations. On top of OffloadFS, we develop OffloadDB, which enables RocksDB to offload MemTable flush and compaction operations, and OffloadPrep, which offloads image pre-processing tasks for machine learning to disaggregated storage nodes. Our evaluation shows that OffloadFS improves the performance of RocksDB and machine learning pre-processing tasks by up to 3.36x and 1.85x, respectively, compared to OCFS2.

</details>
